Do not use for retry-caused duplicates; use make-side-effects-idempotent. ([coder-company/skills](https://github.com/coder-company/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/untangle-shared-state))
- **untangle-this-chat**: Turn an overloaded conversation into a concise, evidence-based overview and identify independent topics suitable for separate chats. Use when the user wants to untangle a complicated chat, decide what matters now, or prepare or explicitly create separate chats for selected topics. ([pengusto/cookbook](https://github.com/pengusto/cookbook/tree/HEAD/skills/untangle-this-chat))
- **untangle-thoughts**: Use when raw, scattered ideas or notes need structure before writing, presenting, or deciding. Triggers on: "생각 정리해줘", "노트 정리", "머릿속이 복잡해", "아이디어 구조화", "outline 만들어줘", "mind map", "이거 어떻게 엮어야 해?", "글 쓰기 전에 구조 잡아줘", "산만한 생각 정리". ([newkayak12/claude-skills](https://github.com/newkayak12/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/think/skills/untangle-thoughts) · ★3)
- **untangling-codebases**: Refactors a whole codebase in one pass so each change lands in one place: catalogs hidden links, needless links, surplus code, and special cases, redesigns to remove their shared causes, and repeats until nothing worth fixing is left. Use when the user says "untangle this codebase", or when asked to refactor a whole codebase so changes land in one place. Never infer from a bug fix or feature request. Not for one new requirement; use redesigning-from-first-principles. ([bostonaholic/skills](https://github.com/bostonaholic/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/untangling-codebases) · ★2)
- **untangling-test-suites**: Workflow for a large, messy, or unmerged pytest suite — inventory it with a deterministic script, classify what to keep, merge, rewrite or delete, decide the target layout and fixture ownership, and cut it into slices with specs that cheap workers can port while the suite stays green. Use when a branch carries hundreds of tests nobody can review, when fixtures are duplicated or shadowed, or before a test-suite refactor is delegated. ([konyklabs/claude-plugins](https://github.com/konyklabs/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/py-testing/skills/untangling-test-suites))
- **untappd-mcp**: Search Untappd beers, breweries, and venues; read user profiles, check-ins, wishlists, distinct beers, badges, friends, and your friend activity feed; and post check-ins, toasts, and comments to your own Untappd account. Use when the user asks about beer ratings, what a beer or brewery is like on Untappd, someone's Untappd check-ins or wishlist, or wants to log/toast/comment on a beer. ([chrischall/untappd-mcp](https://github.com/chrischall/untappd-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/untappd-mcp) · ★1)
- **untar-and-run-report**: Produce an untar and run reproducer archive for an OpenROAD bug, plus paste-ready text for a GitHub issue comment. ([The-OpenROAD-Project/bazel-orfs](https://github.com/The-OpenROAD-Project/bazel-orfs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/untar-and-run-report) · ★31)
- **untard**: Use when: a constraint, process, abstraction, gate, dependency, daemon, or risk-surface is slowing work down and you suspect it earns less than it costs. Trigger to put a thing on trial for its right to exist and rip out net-negative drag. The complement to /upstream — upstream fixes the source of a problem; untard deletes the thing that should never have been a problem. ([jamesnavinhill/luna](https://github.com/jamesnavinhill/luna/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/untard))
- **untargeted-annotation**: Annotate untargeted LC-MS/MS data with libraries and molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/router_collection/workflows/untargeted-annotation) · ★16)
- **untargeted-comparative-metabolomics-preprocessing**: Use when raw centroided LC-HRMS/MS files (mzML/mzXML) from a comparative metabolomics study (e.g. mutant vs. wildtype) need feature detection, alignment, blank removal, and peak-quality filtering into an analysis-ready feature table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-comparative-metabolomics-preprocessing))
- **untargeted-lc-ms-data-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data and need to detect low-quality or mis-integrated peaks in an XCMS-processed xcmsSet object before performing metabolite annotation, statistical analysis, or biomarker discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-lc-ms-data-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-lcms-feature-labeling**: Use when an untargeted LC-MS feature table (XCMS output) contains poorly integrated peaks that manual quality assessment would otherwise need to catch, and you want automated Pass/Fail flagging before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-lcms-feature-labeling))
- **untargeted-lcms-feature-table-processing**: Use when an untargeted LC–MS dataset has already been peak-picked, retention-time corrected, and grouped in XCMS, and you need chromatographic quality features (sharpness, jaggedness, symmetry, modality, etc.) per EIC as classifier input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-lcms-feature-table-processing))
- **untargeted-lcms-peak-annotation**: Use when you have a peak-intensity matrix from untargeted LC-MS analysis (raw detected peaks with m/z and intensity values) and need to assign putative metabolite identities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-lcms-peak-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-lcmsms-annotation-workflow**: Use when you have untargeted LC-MS/MS data (mzML) and want an annotated feature table — preprocessing, blank/QC filtering, feature-based molecular networking, spectral library matching, SIRIUS de novo annotation, optional taxonomy-aware re-weighting, and a fused master table. This is the canonical metabopipe-style annotation pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/workflows/untargeted-lcmsms-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-mass-increment-window-search**: Use when the input is an LC-MS peak table (feature list with m/z, rt, intensities) and candidate homologue mass increments are unknown, so a bounded scan of mass differences (e.g. mz_min = 10 to mz_max = 20) must reveal series automatically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-mass-increment-window-search))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-annotation**: Use when you have authentic metabolite standards analyzed by LC-MS in both positive and negative ESI modes (converted to . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-annotation-validation**: Use when you have IDSL.IPA-generated individual and aligned peak tables (mzXML/mzML/netCDF inputs) and need to validate molecular formula assignments against MS/MS-confirmed identities or report median-rank statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-annotation-validation))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when raw full-scan LC/HRMS data from population-scale untargeted metabolomics or exposomics studies (e.g. mzXML/mzML files from Orbitrap instruments) must be converted into a peak table without MS2 adduct/fragment handling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when you have untargeted MS2 spectral data (from LC-MS/MS or similar instruments) and need to assign metabolic pathway context to detected compounds when standard spectral library matching is unavailable or insufficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when you have two separate LC-MS untargeted metabolomic feature tables (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-data-quality-assessment**: Use when after imputation and signal drift correction (OUKS steps 3–4), when you have a QC-annotated feature intensity table with replicated QC samples and need to assess which features have stable, reproducible signals before filtering and statistical testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-data-quality-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-dataset-integration**: Use when you have two LC-MS feature tables (each with m/z, retention time, and intensity columns) from independent untargeted metabolomic experiments or replicates and need to establish one-to-one feature correspondence across them to compare abundances, detect shared metabolites, or merge datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-dataset-integration) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-dataset-processing**: Use when a batch of untargeted metabolomics datasets (e.g. 20 Orbitrap feature tables + MS2 spectra) must be annotated under competing knowledge-network topologies and compared using decoy-based accuracy, FDR, and same-superclass specificity metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-dataset-processing))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-drug-screening**: Use when untargeted metabolomics MS/MS runs (mzML or converted vendor files) must be screened for drug exposures, especially when only metabolized versions of a drug may be present rather than the parent compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-drug-screening))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-feature-analysis**: Use when you have a feature table from untargeted metabolomics (with m/z, retention time, and p-values from differential abundance testing) but lack or wish to bypass metabolite annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-feature-analysis) · 3 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-feature-extraction**: Use when raw LC/MS mzXML files from untargeted metabolomics need peak picking, quantification and alignment into a feature table prior to statistical feature selection and pathway/network analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-feature-extraction))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-feature-interpretation**: Use when you have an untargeted metabolomics feature table (m/z values, retention times, p-values from statistical testing) and need to infer which metabolic pathways are active without performing metabolite identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-feature-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-feature-scoring**: Use when a set of untargeted metabolomics extracts (GNPS FBMN job + MZmine 2/3 quant table) must be prioritized for structural novelty, with per-extract FC, FS, LC, CC, and SC scores and a final rank. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-feature-scoring))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-feature-table-processing**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics MS1 feature table (.csv with name/mz/rt columns) must be linked onto a curated metabolic reaction network before annotation propagation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-feature-table-processing))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-marker-identification**: Use when when you have untargeted GC–MS or LC–MS data in the form of a two-dimensional m/z vs retention time map and need to identify marker features without conventional peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-marker-identification) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-metabolomics-ordination-analysis**: Use when you have per-tissue untargeted metabolomics peak-area feature tables with sample metadata (study, cohort, genotype) and need to verify whether samples cluster across conditions before running differential or ML analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-ordination-analysis))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-parameter-configuration**: Use when preparing an IDSL.CSA or IDSL.IPA batch run on mzXML/mzML/netCDF LC/HRMS data — a parameter spreadsheet must be filled with workflow selectors (PARAM0001), directory addresses (CSA0005/0008/0009/0011), and thread counts (CSA0004). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-parameter-configuration))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-peak-classifier-inference**: Use when you have an XCMS-processed untargeted LC–MS feature table with peak-quality metrics and want to automatically flag low-quality peak integrations without manual curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-peak-classifier-inference))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-peak-table-generation**: Use when raw LC-MS untargeted metabolomics chromatograms (.CDF files) plus a sample metadata table are available and the goal is to produce a features x samples peak-area/intensity table with retention-time alignment before imputation or correction steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-peak-table-generation))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-preprocessing**: Use when starting from centroided (or raw Thermo/Agilent/Bruker) LC-MS/MS mzML files in an untargeted metabolomics study and you need a per-sample feature table with m/z, RT, adduct, and intensities for downstream FBMN/IIMN or statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-preprocessing))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-processing-parameterization**: Use when raw CE-MS or LC-MS/HILIC-MS metabolomics acquisitions must be processed non-targetedly with AriumMS and per-method processing parameters (ROI thresholds, m/z error, alignment, occurrence filters) must be selected or DOE-optimized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-processing-parameterization))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-raw-data-processing**: Use when raw untargeted metabolomics files (mzML, mzXML, or CDF from qTOF, orbitrap, or FT-ICR instruments) must be processed into a feature table and no universal parameter set applies, so centWave peak-picking parameters must be estimated from the data itself. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-raw-data-processing))
- **untargeted-metabolomics-workflow-implementation**: Use when you have LC-MS/MS data acquired in DDA mode from untargeted metabolomics experiments and need to remove chimeric (co-fragmented) MS/MS spectra that result from multiple precursor ions fragmented simultaneously. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/untargeted-metabolomics-workflow-implementation) · 2 copies)
- **untargeted-mz-window-series-search**: Use when an LC-MS peak table (mz, rt, intensity columns) must be searched for homologue series with an unknown m/z increment: no candidate step (e.g. 44.02628) is available, so a Da-range sweep (mz_min-mz_max) is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/untargeted-mz-window-series-search))
- **untargeted-screening-identification-tallying**: Use when a completed MS-DIAL/MS-FINDER screening yields a list of identified features whose library annotations and unknown IDs must be counted, cross-checked against a reported total (e.g. 83 compounds), and exported as a per-identification-status table. USE when the user wants semantic termination after acting — "fix the build until it passes", "extract issues until none remain", "retry until it succeeds".
